Through experiments on standard VLN benchmarks, we confirm that our method significantly outperforms existing pruning strategies. It successfully preserves superior navigation accuracy under extreme pruning scenarios, all while maintaining the highly competitive inference efficiency. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates reliable and low-latency instruction-following navigation under practical robotic constraints. We hope this work helps bridge the gap between large-scale multimodal modeling and efficient, real-time embodied deployment in robotic navigation systems.

</details>


### [101] [Control Barrier Corridors: From Safety Functions to Safe Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06494)
*Ömür Arslan,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的控制屏障走廊概念，将控制屏障函数和运动安全走廊两种安全方法统一起来，用于自主机器人在未知环境中的安全路径跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制屏障函数和运动安全走廊是两种广泛使用但技术不同的安全方法，分别属于功能性和几何性方法。论文旨在统一这两种方法，以实现在复杂非结构化环境中更有效的安全运动规划和控制。

Method: 提出控制屏障走廊的新概念，将控制屏障函数转换为局部安全目标区域，用于反馈控制系统中的参考目标选择。通过完全驱动系统、运动学独轮车和线性输出调节系统的示例进行验证。

Result: 对于凸屏障函数，只要控制收敛速率与屏障衰减速率匹配，个体状态安全性可以在控制屏障走廊上局部扩展。这揭示了安全性和反应性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 控制屏障走廊实现了在系统运动期间连续变化的屏障走廊上安全可达的持续目标选择，为未知环境中的自主探索提供了可验证的安全和持续路径跟踪能力。

Abstract: Safe autonomy is a critical requirement and a key enabler for robots to operate safely in unstructured complex environments. Control barrier functions and safe motion corridors are two widely used but technically distinct safety methods, functional and geometric, respectively, for safe motion planning and control. Control barrier functions are applied to the safety filtering of control inputs to limit the decay rate of system safety, whereas safe motion corridors are geometrically constructed to define a local safe zone around the system state for use in motion optimization and reference-governor design. This paper introduces a new notion of control barrier corridors, which unifies these two approaches by converting control barrier functions into local safe goal regions for reference goal selection in feedback control systems. We show, with examples on fully actuated systems, kinematic unicycles, and linear output regulation systems, that individual state safety can be extended locally over control barrier corridors for convex barrier functions, provided the control convergence rate matches the barrier decay rate, highlighting a trade-off between safety and reactiveness. Such safe control barrier corridors enable safely reachable persistent goal selection over continuously changing barrier corridors during system motion, which we demonstrate for verifiably safe and persistent path following in autonomous exploration of unknown environments.

</details>


### [102] [CFEAR-Teach-and-Repeat: Fast and Accurate Radar-only Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06501)
*Maximilian Hilger,Daniel Adolfsson,Ralf Becker,Henrik Andreasson,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CFEAR-TR：一种基于单旋转雷达的教导-重复定位系统，专为恶劣天气条件下的鲁棒导航设计，通过联合对齐实时扫描与存储地图及滑动窗口关键帧，实现高精度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶劣天气条件下，光学传感器可能失效，因此需要可靠的地图先验定位方法。雷达传感器在恶劣天气中表现更稳定，但现有雷达定位方法在精度和鲁棒性方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 使用单旋转雷达构建教导-重复定位管道。定位时联合对齐实时扫描与存储的教导地图扫描以及实时关键帧滑动窗口。雷达扫描表示为从多普勒补偿测量计算得到的稀疏定向表面点集。地图存储在姿态图中，定位时遍历该图。

Result: 在Boreas数据集的测试序列上，CFEAR-TR实现了0.117米和0.096°的定位精度，相比先前最优方法提升高达63%。系统运行效率为29Hz，大幅缩小了与激光雷达定位的差距，特别是在航向估计方面。

Conclusion: CFEAR-TR提供了一种轻量级、易于部署且鲁棒的雷达定位解决方案，在恶劣天气条件下表现出色，接近激光雷达的定位性能，为自动驾驶导航提供了可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Reliable localization in prior maps is essential for autonomous navigation, particularly under adverse weather, where optical sensors may fail. We present CFEAR-TR, a teach-and-repeat localization pipeline using a single spinning radar, which is designed for easily deployable, lightweight, and robust navigation in adverse conditions. Our method localizes by jointly aligning live scans to both stored scans from the teach mapping pass, and to a sliding window of recent live keyframes. This ensures accurate and robust pose estimation across different seasons and weather phenomena. Radar scans are represented using a sparse set of oriented surface points, computed from Doppler-compensated measurements. The map is stored in a pose graph that is traversed during localization. Experiments on the held-out test sequences from the Boreas dataset show that CFEAR-TR can localize with an accuracy as low as 0.117 m and 0.096°, corresponding to improvements of up to 63% over the previous state of the art, while running efficiently at 29 Hz. These results substantially narrow the gap to lidar-level localization, particularly in heading estimation. We make the C++ implementation of our work available to the community.

</details>


### [103] [Underactuated multimodal jumping robot for extraterrestrial exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06525)
*Neil R. Wagner,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 设计了一种仅使用三个执行器的欠驱动单腿机器人，通过滚动和跳跃在低重力天体上进行多模态运动，使用两个反作用轮控制空间方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低重力天体上的多模态运动能力，开发一种能够适应不同地形和环境条件的紧凑型机器人系统

Method: 使用仅有两个反作用轮和三个执行器的欠驱动单腿机器人设计，包含两种控制器：地面平衡控制器用于瞄准跳跃方向，空中重定向控制器用于瞄准着陆腿姿态

Result: 成功演示了滚动、目标跳跃和着陆以及自校正功能，系统尺寸为0.33米，重量1.25公斤，能够在不同地形和环境条件下进行简单模式切换

Conclusion: 通过简单的多模态运动切换，紧凑的欠驱动单腿机器人系统能够在低重力天体上有效应对不同的地形和环境条件

Abstract: We present a rolling and jumping underactuated monopedal robot designed to explore multimodal locomotion on low-gravity bodies. It uses only two reaction wheels to control its spatial orientation with two controllers: a balancing controller which can aim the robot's jump direction on the ground, and an aerial reorientation controller which can aim the robot's leg for landing after flight. We demonstrate rolling, targeted jumping and landing, and self-righting using only three actuators total, keeping system size to 0.33m and 1.25kg. Simple switching between locomotion modes enables the system to deal with differing landscapes and environmental conditions.

</details>


### [104] [Unified Learning of Temporal Task Structure and Action Timing for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06538)
*Christian Dreher,Patrick Dormanns,Andre Meixner,Tamim Asfour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从人类演示中学习符号和次符号时间任务约束的方法，用于双手机器人操作的时序参数化规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作需要同时考虑高层次的时间关系推理和具体的时序参数协调，现有方法在这两个层面孤立处理，导致高层任务规划与低层运动同步之间存在鸿沟

Method: 1) 使用三维时序表示和多元高斯混合模型学习动作间的次符号时间关系；2) 基于DPLL算法寻找并排序所有无矛盾的Allen关系分配，表示任务的不同模式；3) 结合符号和次符号约束的优化规划系统，生成时序参数化执行计划

Result: 在多个数据集上评估，该方法生成的时序参数化计划比最具代表性的演示基线更接近人类演示

Conclusion: 该方法成功填补了高层任务规划与低层运动同步之间的鸿沟，能够从人类演示中学习并生成可执行的时序参数化双手机器人操作计划

Abstract: Temporal task structure is fundamental for bimanual manipulation: a robot must not only know that one action precedes or overlaps another, but also when each action should occur and how long it should take. While symbolic temporal relations enable high-level reasoning about task structure and alternative execution sequences, concrete timing parameters are equally essential for coordinating two hands at the execution level. Existing approaches address these two levels in isolation, leaving a gap between high-level task planning and low-level movement synchronization. This work presents an approach for learning both symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints from human demonstrations and deriving executable, temporally parametrized plans for bimanual manipulation. Our contributions are (i) a 3-dimensional representation of timings between two actions with methods based on multivariate Gaussian Mixture Models to represent temporal relationships between actions on a subsymbolic level, (ii) a method based on the Davis-Putnam-Logemann-Loveland (DPLL) algorithm that finds and ranks all contradiction-free assignments of Allen relations to action pairs, representing different modes of a task, and (iii) an optimization-based planning system that combines the identified symbolic and subsymbolic temporal task constraints to derive temporally parametrized plans for robot execution. We evaluate our approach on several datasets, demonstrating that our method generates temporally parametrized plans closer to human demonstrations than the most characteristic demonstration baseline.

</details>


### [105] [Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Dynamics For Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06548)
*Edward Morgan,Nenyi K Dadson,Corina Barbalata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知自适应动力学模型框架，用于水下车辆-机械臂系统，通过移动时域估计在线更新参数并保证物理一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下车辆-机械臂系统受时变水动力效应影响，需要准确且自适应的动态模型来应对参数变化

Method: 采用线性参数化模型，结合移动时域估计方法，在在线估计中嵌入凸物理一致性约束（惯性、阻尼、摩擦、水静力学），并量化参数演化的不确定性

Result: 在BlueROV2 Heavy和4自由度机械臂上实验验证：机械臂拟合R²=0.88-0.98，车辆纵荡、垂荡和横摇运动再现良好；中值求解时间约0.023秒；相比固定参数模型，各自由度MAE和RMSE均降低；参数物理合理，置信区间覆盖率近100%

Conclusion: 该框架能实现快速收敛、校准良好的预测和物理合理的参数估计，为水下环境中的可靠前馈控制和仿真提供了可行方案

Abstract: Accurate and adaptive dynamic models are critical for underwater vehicle-manipulator systems where hydrodynamic effects induce time-varying parameters. This paper introduces a novel uncertainty-aware adaptive dynamics model framework that remains linear in lumped vehicle and manipulator parameters, and embeds convex physical consistency constraints during online estimation. Moving horizon estimation is used to stack horizon regressors, enforce realizable inertia, damping, friction, and hydrostatics, and quantify uncertainty from parameter evolution. Experiments on a BlueROV2 Heavy with a 4-DOF manipulator demonstrate rapid convergence and calibrated predictions. Manipulator fits achieve R2 = 0.88 to 0.98 with slopes near unity, while vehicle surge, heave, and roll are reproduced with good fidelity under stronger coupling and noise. Median solver time is approximately 0.023 s per update, confirming online feasibility. A comparison against a fixed parameter model shows consistent reductions in MAE and RMSE across degrees of freedom. Results indicate physically plausible parameters and confidence intervals with near 100% coverage, enabling reliable feedforward control and simulation in underwater environments.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [The fourth known primitive solution to $a^5 + b^5 + c^5 + d^5 = e^5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05549)
*Jeffrey Braun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 发现第五次幂和方程a⁵+b⁵+c⁵+d⁵=e⁵的第四个已知本原解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找第五次幂和方程a⁵+b⁵+c⁵+d⁵=e⁵的新本原解，扩展该数论问题的已知解集

Method: 开发了新的搜索方法学，可能包括算法优化、计算策略或数论技术来寻找满足方程的正整数解

Result: 成功发现了该方程的第四个已知本原解，为这个长期研究的数论问题提供了新的具体实例

Conclusion: 提出的搜索方法有效，新解丰富了第五次幂和方程的解集，为进一步研究此类丢番图方程提供了新思路

Abstract: We present a new primitive solution to the Diophantine equation $a^5 + b^5 + c^5 + d^5 = e^5$, along with our search methodology and results. This is the fourth known primitive solution to this equation.

</details>


### [107] [Cohen-Macaulayness of Local Models via Shellability of the Admissible Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05875)
*Xuhua He,Felix Schremmer,Qingchao Yu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了增广容许集的双重EL-可壳性，解决了Görtz猜想，为抛物水平局部模型提供了新的Cohen-Macaulay性质证明，覆盖了特征2和非约化根系等开放情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Görtz关于增广容许集双重EL-可壳性的猜想，为抛物水平局部模型的特殊纤维提供新的Cohen-Macaulay性质证明方法，特别是处理特征2和非约化根系等先前开放的情形。

Method: 采用特征无关的方法，基于容许集的内在结构，构造显式的壳化过程，转化为特殊纤维的逐分量归纳构建过程，每一步都保持Cohen-Macaulay性质。

Result: 证明了对于任意主导余特征μ和任意抛物水平K，Iwahori-Weyl群中的增广容许集是双重EL-可壳的，从而解决了Görtz猜想，并为抛物水平局部模型的特殊纤维提供了新的Cohen-Macaulay性质证明。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了增广容许集的双重EL-可壳性，解决了Görtz猜想，为抛物水平局部模型的Cohen-Macaulay性质提供了特征无关的证明，覆盖了特征2和非约化根系等先前开放情形，并由此推导出许多志村簇局部模型及其积分模型的Cohen-Macaulay性质。

Abstract: We prove that for any dominant cocharacter $μ$ and any parahoric level $K$, the augmented admissible set $\widehat{\Adm(μ)^K}$ in the Iwahori-Weyl group is dual EL-shellable. This resolves a conjecture of Görtz and provides a new proof of the Cohen-Macaulay property for the special fibres of local models with parahoric level structure. In particular, the result settles the previously open cases of residue characteristic $2$ and non-reduced root systems.
  This approach is characteristic-free and intrinsic to the structure of admissible sets. Moreover, our construction yields an explicit shelling, which translates into an inductive, component-by-component building procedure for the special fibre that preserves Cohen-Macaulayness at each step.
  As a consequence, we obtain the Cohen-Macaulayness of many local models of Shimura varieties considered in the literature, most notably those satisfying the He-Pappas-Rapoport description, as well as the local models characterized by Scholze-Weinstein and constructed by Anschütz-Gleason-Lourenço-Richarz. Via the usual local model diagram, these results imply the Cohen-Macaulay property for the corresponding integral models of Shimura varieties whenever available. This gives a new proof that the integral models constructed by Kisin-Pappas-Zhou are Cohen-Macaulay.

</details>


### [108] [Waring-Goldbach problems for one square and higher powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05550)
*Geovane Matheus Lemes Andrade*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了每个充分大的奇数可以表示为一个平方与十四个五次方素数之和，每个充分大的偶数可以表示为一个平方、一个四次方与十二个五次方素数之和


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数的加性表示问题，特别是将大整数表示为素数幂次之和的形式，这是解析数论中的经典问题，扩展了华林-哥德巴赫型问题的研究范围

Method: 采用解析数论方法，可能包括圆法、筛法、指数和估计等技术，通过处理素数幂次和的表示问题来证明存在性结果

Result: 证明了两个主要定理：1) 每个充分大的奇数可以表示为 p₁² + p₂⁵ + ... + p₁₅⁵（共14个五次方素数）；2) 每个充分大的偶数可以表示为 p₁² + p₂⁴ + p₃⁵ + ... + p₁₄⁵（共12个五次方素数）

Conclusion: 该研究在素数幂次表示问题上取得了新的进展，为华林-哥德巴赫型问题提供了更精细的结果，将整数表示为更少素数幂次组合的可能性

Abstract: We prove that every sufficiently large odd integer can be expressed as a sum of one square and fourteen fifth powers, all of primes. In addition, we establish that every sufficiently large even integer can be written as a sum of one square, one biquadrate, and twelve fifth powers of primes.

</details>


### [109] [Ramanujan Complexes from Unitary Groups over Number Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06156)
*Rahul Dalal,Alberto Mínguez,Jiandi Zou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文构造了具有新局部结构的Ramanujan复形无限族，基于超定酉群，包含A_n、^2A'_n、^2A''_n、B-C_n、^2B-C_n和C-BC_n等类型的新例子，并在秩5情形给出了完全显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有新局部结构的Ramanujan复形，这些复形在图论和计算机科学中有重要应用，特别是作为扩展子图的高维推广。现有构造的局部结构有限，需要新的构造方法来丰富Ramanujan复形的多样性。

Method: 基于数域上的酉群，特别是超定酉群（在有限位点模中心后各向同性的定酉群）。这些群自然地出现在带第二类对合的中央除代数的单位群中。构造依赖于完全实数域上的超定酉群G和有限位点v_0，所得复形的结构由v_0处的Bruhat-Tits大楼类型决定。

Result: 1. 给出了与超定酉群相关的Ramanujan复形无限族的一般构造；2. 当v_0分裂时得到A_n类型的新例子；3. 在非分裂情形得到^2A'_n、^2A''_n（n偶）、B-C_n、^2B-C_n和C-BC_n类型的新族；4. 构造在所有秩上一致工作；5. 在秩5情形给出了完全显式构造，为实李群PU(5)提供了黄金门。

Conclusion: 该论文成功构造了具有丰富局部结构多样性的新Ramanujan复形族，扩展了已知的Ramanujan复形类型。通过超定酉群方法，实现了在分裂和非分裂情形下的统一构造，并在算法显式性方面取得进展，为计算机科学应用提供了具体实例。

Abstract: In this article, we construct new families of Ramanujan complexes with local structure distinct from all previously known examples. Our approach is based on unitary groups over number fields, more specifically on what we call super-definite unitary groups, that is definite unitary groups that are anisotropic modulo their center at a finite place. These arise naturally as groups of units in central division algebras with involution of the second kind.
  Our first main result gives a general construction of infinite families of Ramanujan complexes associated with a super-definite unitary group $G$ over a totally real number field and a finite place $v_0$. The structure of the resulting complex is governed by the type of the Bruhat-Tits building at $v_0$. It includes new examples of type $A_n$ when $v_0$ is split, and novel families of type ${}^2\!A'_n$, ${}^2 \! A''_n$ (with $n$ even), $B$-$C_n$, ${}^2 \! B$-$C_n$ and $C$-$BC_n$ in the non-split case. This construction works uniformly across all ranks.
  Since much of the motivation for constructing expander complexes comes from computer science, we investigate the algorithmic explicitness of our construction in the latter part of the paper, and provide an example in rank 5 where it becomes fully explicit. In particular, this example yields golden gates for the real Lie group $PU(5)$.

</details>


### [110] [Joint Linnik problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05609)
*Valentin Blomer,Farrell Brumley,Maksym Radiwiłł*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了Michel-Venkatesh关于不同Linnik问题连接的一个猜想，适用于具有足够多小分裂素数的虚二次域的同时四元数嵌入情形


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Michel-Venkatesh关于不同Linnik问题连接的猜想，以及Aka-Einsiedler-Shapira提出的非等变形式，这些连接问题在数论和自守形式理论中具有重要意义

Method: 利用虚二次域的同时四元数嵌入方法，要求域具有足够多的小分裂素数，该分裂条件对几乎所有判别式都成立

Result: 证明了Michel-Venkatesh猜想，并处理了Aka-Einsiedler-Shapira的非等变形式，特别适用于球面上Linnik点与模曲面CM点的经典Gauss构造连接

Conclusion: 在满足分裂条件的虚二次域上，成功建立了不同Linnik问题之间的连接，为相关数论问题的研究提供了重要工具

Abstract: We prove a conjecture of Michel--Venkatesh on joinings of distinct Linnik problems, in the setting of simultaneous quaternionic embeddings of imaginary quadratic fields having sufficiently many small split primes. This splitting condition is known to hold for all but $O((\log\log X)^{1 + o(1)})$ discriminants up to $X$. We also treat a non-equivariant form of this conjecture proposed by Aka--Einsiedler--Shapira, which in particular applies to the classical Gauss construction joining Linnik points on the sphere with CM points on the modular surface.

</details>


### [111] [On an elementary method for solving $Ax^4-By^2=1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05809)
*P. G. Walsh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究Luo和Lin提出的四次方程新解法，应用于解决Bumby方程3X⁴-2Y²=1，并提出一个猜想，该猜想若被证明可推广到无限族类似方程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Luo和Lin提出的四次方程新解法在理论和计算上的可行性，并寻找解决特定丢番图方程（如Bumby方程）的初等方法

Method: 对Luo-Lin四次方程解法进行理论和计算分析，应用该方法推导出解决Bumby方程3X⁴-2Y²=1的初等解法，并提出一个关于解结构的猜想

Result: 成功给出了解决Bumby方程的完全初等直接方法，并提出了一个猜想，该猜想若成立可将类似证明推广到可能无限的方程族

Conclusion: Luo-Lin方法为解决特定类型四次丢番图方程提供了有效途径，提出的猜想为进一步研究无限族类似方程开辟了方向

Abstract: A new method for solving quartic equations due to Luo and Lin is investigated both computationally and theoretically. As a result, a completely straightforward elementary method is given for solving Bumby's equation $3X^4-2Y^2=1$, along with a conjecture, which if resolved, would enable a similar proof for a possibly infinite family of similar equations.

</details>


### [112] [On indefinite integral ternary quadratic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05849)
*Alexander Gamburd,Amit Ghosh,Peter Sarnak,Junho Peter Whang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Margulis和Serre在1990年提出的两个关于不定三元二次型积分的问题，通过开发处理高分歧情况下加权求和的新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Margulis和Serre在1990年提出的关于不定三元二次型积分的两个长期未决问题，这些问题是该领域的经典难题。

Method: 开发了处理高分歧情况的新工具，用于处理加权于其丢番图不变量的不定三元二次型类上的求和问题。

Result: 成功解决了Margulis和Serre提出的两个问题，为处理高分歧情况下的二次型求和问题提供了有效方法。

Conclusion: 通过开发处理高分歧的新技术，论文成功解决了数论中关于不定三元二次型积分的两个经典问题，为相关领域的研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We resolve two problems pertaining to indefinite integral ternary quadratic forms, one highlighted by Margulis and the other initiated by Serre, both from 1990. To do so we develop tools for dealing with high ramification in problems involving sums over classes of such forms weighted by their diophantine invariants.

</details>


### [113] [On the spectrum of Diophantine exponents of lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06016)
*Oleg N. German*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意维度中格点弱一致丢番图指数的值谱


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究格点弱一致丢番图指数的可能取值范围，这是丢番图逼近理论中的一个基本问题

Method: 使用丢番图逼近理论的方法，分析格点弱一致丢番图指数的性质

Result: 确定了任意维度中格点弱一致丢番图指数的完整值谱

Conclusion: 完全刻画了格点弱一致丢番图指数在任意维度中的可能取值范围

Abstract: In this paper we describe the spectrum of values of weak uniform Diophantine exponents of lattices in arbitrary dimension.

</details>


### [114] [On The Hausdorff Dimension Of Two Dimensional Badly Approximable Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06137)
*Yi Lou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文研究了加权Ψ-坏逼近向量集的豪斯多夫维数，在加权幂律设置下建立了二维情况的精确局部豪斯多夫维数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加权逼近函数Ψ下Ψ-坏逼近向量集的豪斯多夫维数，特别是从非加权情况推广到加权情况，建立精确的维数公式。

Method: 将Koivusalo、Levesley、Ward和Zhang的康托型构造和质量分布论证从非加权情况扩展到加权情况，独立于加权精确逼近的最新结果。

Result: 当τ=(τ₁,τ₂)满足τ₁≥τ₂>0且τ₁+τ₂>1时，对于[0,1]²中的任意球B，建立了加权Ψ-坏逼近向量集的豪斯多夫维数公式：dim_H(B∩B₂(Ψ_τ)) = dim_H A₂(Ψ_τ) = min{(3+τ₁-τ₂)/(1+τ₁), 3/(1+τ₂)}。

Conclusion: 成功将非加权情况的豪斯多夫维数计算方法推广到加权情况，建立了加权幂律设置下二维Ψ-坏逼近向量集的精确局部豪斯多夫维数公式。

Abstract: Let $Ψ=(ψ_1,\dots,ψ_m)$ be an approximation function and denote by $\mathcal{A}_m(Ψ)$ the set of $Ψ$-approximable vectors in $[0,1]^m$. The associated set of weighted $Ψ$-badly approximable vectors is defined by $$\mathcal{B}_m(Ψ)=\mathcal{A}_m(Ψ)\setminus\bigcap \limits_{0<c<1}\mathcal{A}_m(cΨ).$$ In this paper, we study the Hausdorff dimension of $\mathcal{B}_m(Ψ)$ in the weighted power-law setting $ψ_i(q)=q^{-τ_i}$. Our main result establishes a sharp local Hausdorff dimension formula for $\mathcal{B}_2(Ψ_{\boldsymbolτ})$ when $\boldsymbolτ=(τ_1,τ_2)$ satisfies $τ_1\geqτ_2>0$ and $τ_1+τ_2>1$. We show that for any ball $B\subseteq[0,1]^2$, $$\dim_{\mathcal{H}}(B\cap \mathcal{B}_2(Ψ_{\boldsymbol τ})) = \dim_{\mathcal{H}} \mathcal{A}_2(Ψ_{\boldsymbol τ}) =\min \left\{\frac{3+τ_1-τ_2}{1+τ_1},\frac{3}{1+τ_2}\right\}.$$ The proof extends the Cantor-type construction and mass distribution arguments of Koivusalo, Levesley, Ward, and Zhang from the unweighted to the weighted setting, and is independent of recent results on weighted exact approximation.

</details>


### [115] [Rational Preperiodic Points of Quadratic Rational Maps over $\mathbb{Q}$ with Nonabelian Automorphism Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06203)
*Hasan Bilgili,Mohammad Sadek*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有非阿贝尔自同构群的二次有理映射在有理数域上的周期点分类，证明了周期1、2、3的有理周期点的完全分类，以及不存在周期4或5的有理周期点，并给出了预周期点的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有理数域上具有非阿贝尔自同构群的二次有理映射的周期点结构，这类映射在算术动力学中具有重要意义，因为它们的自同构群性质会影响周期点的分布和存在性。

Method: 采用代数几何和算术动力学的方法，首先对具有非阿贝尔自同构群的二次有理映射进行分类，然后通过分析这些映射的特定形式，利用代数方程和数论工具来研究有理周期点的存在性和分类。

Result: 1. 完全分类了具有非阿贝尔自同构群的二次有理映射在有理数域上的周期1、2、3的有理周期点；2. 证明了这类映射不存在周期4或5的有理周期点；3. 证明了如果这类映射没有周期大于3的有理周期点，则其有理预周期点的数量最多为6个。

Conclusion: 具有非阿贝尔自同构群的二次有理映射在有理数域上的周期点结构受到严格限制：低周期点（1、2、3）可完全分类，中等周期点（4、5）不存在，且预周期点数量有明确上界，这为理解这类映射的算术动力学性质提供了重要结果。

Abstract: Let $f:\mathbb{P}^1 \rightarrow \mathbb{P}^1$ be a quadratic rational map defined over the rational field $\mathbb Q$ with nonabelian automorphism group. We completely classify such maps that have $\mathbb Q$-rational periodic points of period $1$, $2$, and $3$. We then prove that no such map has a $\mathbb Q$-rational periodic point of exact period $4$ or $5$. We also show that if such a map has no $\mathbb Q$-rational periodic points of exact period exceeding $3$, then the number of its $\mathbb Q$-rational preperiodic points is at most $6$.

</details>


### [116] [On the monogenicity and Galois groups of $\boldsymbol{x^{2p}+ax^p+b^p}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06432)
*Joshua Harrington,Lenny Jones*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了形如f(x)=x^{2p}+ax^p+b^p的不可约三项式的单生性，其中p为素数，a,b为非零整数。文章根据Galois群给出了这类三项式单生性的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究特定形式的三项式（x^{2p}+ax^p+b^p）的单生性条件，扩展作者先前的工作，建立Galois群与单生性之间的明确关系。

Method: 通过代数数论方法，分析三项式的Galois群结构，推导出单生性的判别条件。利用分圆域理论和整数环基的性质，建立Galois群类型与单生性之间的对应关系。

Result: 给出了这类三项式单生性的完整刻画：根据Galois群的不同类型（如循环群、二面体群等），确定了相应的单生性条件。这些结果推广了作者先前的研究。

Conclusion: 对于形如x^{2p}+ax^p+b^p的三项式，其单生性完全由Galois群决定。该研究建立了Galois群理论与单生性之间的深刻联系，为这类多项式的算术性质提供了系统分类。

Abstract: Let $f(x)=x^{2p}+ax^p+b^p$, where $p$ is a prime and $a,b\in {\mathbb Z}$ with $ab\ne 0$. If $f(x)$ is irreducible over ${\mathbb Q}$, we say that $f(x)$ is monogenic if $\{1,θ,θ^2,\ldots ,θ^{2p-1}\}$
  is a basis for the ring of integers of ${\mathbb Q}(θ)$, where $f(θ)=0$.
  In this article, we give a characterization of the monogenic trinomials $f(x)$ according to their Galois groups. These results extend prior investigations of the authors.

</details>


### [117] [On the integer partitions recursive structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06438)
*Boris Y. Rubinstein*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: Sylvester证明了整数分拆可表示为多项式项与准周期分量（Sylvester波）之和，波本身是多项式项乘以周期函数的加权和，整数权重可表示为更小整数集合的分拆和，揭示了整数分拆的递归结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索整数分拆的数学结构，特别是Sylvester波的表示方法，以揭示整数分拆的内在递归性质和准周期特征。

Method: 采用Sylvester的整数分拆理论，将分拆表示为多项式项与准周期分量（Sylvester波）之和，波本身是多项式项乘以周期函数的加权和，通过分析整数权重与更小整数集合分拆的关系来揭示递归结构。

Result: 发现整数分拆可分解为多项式项和准周期分量，波中的整数权重可表示为更小整数集合的分拆和，这证明了整数分拆具有递归结构，权重本身也遵循分拆规律。

Conclusion: Sylvester的整数分拆理论揭示了分拆的递归本质：整数分拆可表示为多项式项与准周期分量之和，且权重本身也是分拆和，建立了整数分拆的层次化递归结构。

Abstract: Sylvester showed that the partition of an integer into a set of positive integers can be represented as a sum of the polynomial term and quasiperiodic components called the Sylvester waves. The wave itself is a weighted sum of the polynomial terms multiplied by the periodic functions. The integer weights are found to be a sum of partitions into a smaller set of integers implying the recursive structure of integer partitions.

</details>


### [118] [Uniform sum-product phenomenon for algebraic groups and Bremner's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06483)
*Joseph Harrison,Akshat Mudgal,Harry Schmidt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 结合加法组合与丢番图几何研究代数群中的广义和积现象，解决Bremner猜想及相关问题，证明一致Bourgain-Chang型和积估计，提供Bays-Breuillard问题的替代解，改进Elekes-Szabó型结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数群中的广义和积现象，解决椭圆曲线坐标中算术级数的Bremner猜想，以及文献中研究的相关推广问题

Method: 结合加法组合与丢番图几何方法，使用David-Philippon、Laurent、Evertse-Schmidt-Schlickewei的经典丢番图几何结果，以及Gowers-Green-Manners-Tao在整数上弱多项式Freiman-Ruzsa猜想的突破性工作

Result: 1) 解决椭圆曲线坐标中算术级数的Bremner猜想；2) 证明复数域上一般1维代数群的一致Bourgain-Chang型和积估计；3) 提供Bays-Breuillard问题的替代解；4) 对小倍集改进Elekes-Szabó型结果，当G不是加法群时优于Bays-Breuillard早期结果，且幂节省在数量上最优

Conclusion: 通过结合加法组合与丢番图几何方法，成功解决了代数群中广义和积现象的一系列重要问题，包括Bremner猜想、Bourgain-Chang型估计、Bays-Breuillard问题以及改进的Elekes-Szabó型结果，展示了这些交叉方法在研究代数结构算术性质中的强大威力

Abstract: In this paper we combine methods from additive combinatorics and Diophantine geometry to study the generalised sum-product phenomenon in algebraic groups. As an application of this circle of ideas, we resolve a conjecture of Bremner on arithmetic progressions in coordinates of elliptic curves, along with various other generalisations studied in the literature.
  We also prove a uniform Bourgain--Chang-type sum-product estimate for general $1$-dimensional algebraic groups $G$ over $\mathbb{C}$. Using these ideas, we provide an alternative solution to a problem of Bays--Breuillard. Furthermore, we show an Elekes--Szabó type result in the same setting for sets with small doubling, improving upon an earlier result of Bays--Breuillard when $G$ is not $\mathbb{G}_a$. Our power saving here can be shown to be quantitatively optimal.
  We use a combination of deep, classical results in Diophantine geometry due to David--Philippon, Laurent and Evertse--Schmidt--Schlickewei along with the recent breakthrough work on the weak Polynomial Freiman--Ruzsa conjecture over integers due to Gowers--Green--Manners--Tao.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Recognizing Subgraphs of Regular Tilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06367)
*Eliel Ingervo,Sándor Kisfaludi-Bak*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了{p,q}-铺砌图的子图同构识别问题，针对球面、欧几里得平面和双曲平面三种几何情形，分别给出了不同的算法复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究{p,q}-铺砌图的子图同构识别问题的计算复杂度，探索不同几何结构（球面、欧几里得、双曲）对算法效率的影响，特别是双曲铺砌图的特殊性质能否带来更高效的算法。

Method: 针对三种几何情形采用不同方法：球面情形为常数时间；欧几里得情形使用分治算法获得亚指数时间；双曲情形利用凸包性质和球面切割分解的动态规划算法，达到拟多项式时间。

Result: 1) 球面铺砌图：子图识别为常数时间；2) 欧几里得铺砌图：分治算法获得亚指数时间，且在T_{4,4}中存在指数时间假设下的匹配下界；3) 双曲铺砌图：对任意固定q，可在拟多项式时间(n^{O(log n)})内判定。

Conclusion: 双曲铺砌图的子图同构问题复杂度显著低于欧几里得情形，不太可能是NP-hard问题。欧几里得情形即使对树输入也可能是最大难度的。双曲铺砌图的凸包性质为设计高效算法提供了关键技术支持。

Abstract: For $p,q\ge2$ the $\{p,q\}$-tiling graph is the (finite or infinite) planar graph $T_{p,q}$ where all faces are cycles of length $p$ and all vertices have degree $q$. We give algorithms for the problem of recognizing (induced) subgraphs of these graphs, as follows.
  - For $1/p+1/q>1/2$, these graphs correspond to regular tilings of the sphere. These graphs are finite, thus recognizing their (induced) subgraphs can be done in constant time.
  - For $1/p+1/q=1/2$, these graphs correspond to regular tilings of the Euclidean plane. For the Euclidean square grid $T_{4,4}$ Bhatt and Cosmadakis (IPL'87) showed that recognizing subgraphs is NP-hard, even if the input graph is a tree. We show that a simple divide-and conquer algorithm achieves a subexponential running time in all Euclidean tilings, and we observe that there is an almost matching lower bound in $T_{4,4}$ under the Exponential Time Hypothesis via known reductions.
  - For $1/p+1/q<1/2$, these graphs correspond to regular tilings of the hyperbolic plane. As our main contribution, we show that deciding if an $n$-vertex graph is isomorphic to a subgraph of the tiling $T_{p,q}$ can be done in quasi-polynomial ($n^{O(\log n)}$) time for any fixed $q$.
  Our results for the hyperbolic case show that it has significantly lower complexity than the Euclidean variant, and it is unlikely to be NP-hard. The Euclidean results also suggest that the problem can be maximally hard even if the graph in question is a tree. Consequently, the known treewidth bounds for subgraphs of hyperbolic tilings do not lead to an efficient algorithm by themselves. Instead, we use convex hulls within the tiling graph, which have several desirable properties in hyperbolic tilings. Our key technical insight is that planar subgraph isomorphism can be computed via a dynamic program that builds a sphere cut decomposition of a solution subgraph's convex hull.

</details>

H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次通过多通道联合分析确定奇异粲偶素态T_{cc̄}(4020)的性质，通过e⁺e⁻→π⁺T_{cc̄}(4020)⁻+c.c.过程，基于BESIII探测器数据，确定其自旋宇称为Jᴾ=1⁺，并提取了相对分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奇异粲偶素态T_{cc̄}(4020)的性质，特别是其自旋宇称量子数和衰变特性，以增进对这类奇特强子态的理解。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器在√s=4.395和4.416 GeV采集的1598.9 pb⁻¹数据，对e⁺e⁻→π⁺T_{cc̄}(4020)⁻+c.c.过程进行多通道联合分析。同时分析三个衰变通道：T_{cc̄}(4020)⁻→D*⁰D*-、π⁻J/ψ和π⁻h_c，采用分波分析方法，在复能平面上提取极点位置。

Result: 首次确定T_{cc̄}(4020)⁻的自旋宇称为Jᴾ=1⁺，显著性达11.7σ。获得相对分支比：ℬ[π⁻J/ψ]/ℬ[D*⁰D*-]=(3.6±0.6±1.6)×10⁻³，ℬ[π⁻h_c]/ℬ[D*⁰D*-]=(8.9±1.3±2.3)×10⁻²。在复能平面上提取了极点位置。

Conclusion: 通过多通道联合分析成功确定了T_{cc̄}(4020)⁻的自旋宇称量子数和相对衰变分支比，为理解这一奇异粲偶素态的性质提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: This paper reports the first multi-channel joint analysis to identify the properties of the exotic charmonium-like state $T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)$ via the electron-positron annihilation process $e^{+}e^{-}\toπ^{+}T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}+c.c$. A partial wave analysis is performed simultaneously in three decay channels $T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}\to {D}^{*0}D^{*-}$, $π^{-}J/ψ$, and $π^{-}h_{c}$, based on data samples taken at $\sqrt{s}=4.395$ and $4.416\,\mathrm{GeV}$ with an integrated luminosity of $1598.9\,\mathrm{pb}^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector operating on the BEPCII collider. For the first time, the spin-parity of the $T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}$ is determined to be $J^{P}=1^{+}$ with a significance $11.7σ$. Pole positions are extracted on the Riemann sheets with three branch points in the complex energy plane. Furthermore, the relative branching fractions are obtained as $\mathcal{B}[T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}\toπ^{-}J/ψ]/\mathcal{B}[T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}\to{D}^{*0}D^{*-}]=(3.6\pm0.6\pm1.6)\times10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}[T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}\toπ^{-}h_{c}]/\mathcal{B}[T_{c\bar{c}}(4020)^{-}\to{D}^{*0}D^{*-}]=(8.9\pm1.3\pm2.3)\times10^{-2}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical, and the second are systematic.

</details>


### [121] [Search for the Lepton Flavour Violating decays $Υ(2S)\rightarrow e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}$ and $Υ(3S)\rightarrow e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05799)
*H. Ahmed,N. Tasneem,J. M. Roney*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BABAR实验组使用Υ(2S)和Υ(3S)数据样本搜索带电轻子味破坏过程Υ→e±μ∓衰变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电轻子味破坏过程在标准模型中不可观测，但在多种新物理扩展模型中可能增强，Υ介子衰变为e±μ∓是探测此类新物理的重要通道

Method: 使用BABAR探测器在SLAC PEP-II e+e-对撞机上收集的数据，包含9900万个Υ(2S)和1.22亿个Υ(3S)介子，质心能量分别为10.02和10.36 GeV，搜索Υ→e±μ∓衰变信号

Result: 论文展示了Υ(2S)和Υ(3S)衰变到e±μ∓的搜索结果，但摘要未提供具体观测结果或上限值

Conclusion: 通过分析BABAR实验数据，对Υ介子的带电轻子味破坏衰变进行了搜索，为探测超出标准模型的新物理提供了重要约束

Abstract: Charged lepton flavour violating processes are unobservable in the standard model, but they are predicted to be enhanced in several new physics extensions. We present the results of a search for $Υ(2S)$ and $Υ(3S)$ decays to $e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}$ decays. The search was conducted using data samples consisting of 99 million $Υ(2S)$ and 122 million $Υ(3S)$ mesons, collected at center-of-mass energies of 10.02 and 10.36 GeV, respectively, by the BABAR detector at the SLAC PEP-II $e^{+}e^{-}$ collider.

</details>


### [122] [Transformer-Based Pulse Shape Discrimination in HPGe Detectors with Masked Autoencoder Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06192)
*Marta Babicz,Saúl Alonso-Monsalve,Alain Fauquex,Laura Baudis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于Transformer的模型在HPGe探测器脉冲形状鉴别中优于传统方法，自监督预训练可显著降低标注数据需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脉冲形状鉴别方法将波形压缩为少量参数，可能丢失全时间序列中的重要分类信息。需要开发直接处理数字化波形的深度学习方法，以提升稀有事件搜索（如无中微子双贝塔衰变）的性能。

Method: 使用Majorana Demonstrator AI/ML数据集，训练基于Transformer的模型直接处理数字化波形。比较三种方法：1) 从头开始的监督训练；2) 掩码自编码器自监督预训练后微调；3) 基于特征的梯度提升决策树基线。模型任务包括复现四个标准PSD切割的接受/拒绝标签以及回归校准能量。

Result: Transformer在所有PSD目标上均优于GBDT，在最具挑战性的标签和组合PSD通过定义上提升最大。MAE预训练提高了样本效率，在低标注数据情况下可将标注需求降低2-4倍。对于能量回归，两种Transformer变体在测试集上都显示出轻微的系统性低估，而微调略微缩小了残差分布。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在HPGe探测器脉冲形状鉴别中表现出优越性能，自监督预训练能有效减少标注数据需求。这些结果支持后续研究在不同探测器和操作条件下的鲁棒性，以及在QBB附近的性能表现。

Abstract: Pulse-shape discrimination (PSD) in high-purity germanium (HPGe) detectors is central to rare-event searches such as neutrinoless double-beta decay (0vBB), yet conventional approaches compress each waveform into a small set of summary parameters, potentially discarding information in the full time series that is relevant for classification. We benchmark transformer-based models that operate directly on digitised waveforms using the Majorana Demonstrator AI/ML data release. Models are trained to reproduce the collaboration-provided accept/reject labels for four standard PSD cuts and to regress calibrated energy. We compare supervised training from scratch, masked autoencoder (MAE) self-supervised pre-training followed by fine-tuning, and a feature-based gradient-boosted decision tree (GBDT) baseline. Transformers outperform GBDT across all PSD targets, with the largest gains on the most challenging labels and on the combined PSD-pass definition. MAE pre-training improves sample efficiency, reducing labelled-data requirements by factors of 2-4 in low-label regimes. For energy regression, both transformer variants show a small common underestimation on the test split, while fine-tuning modestly narrows the residual distribution. These results motivate follow-up studies of robustness across detectors and operating conditions and of performance near QBB.

</details>


### [123] [Construction and Science of SURF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06504)
*Jaret Heise*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了桑福德地下研究设施（SURF）的历史、特点、现状、科学项目及未来规划，该设施是全球最深、占地面积最大的地下实验室之一，专注于多学科前沿科学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SURF作为全球最深、占地面积最大的地下实验室之一，为多学科前沿科学研究提供独特的地下环境。其动机是为物理、天体物理等领域的实验提供超低本底环境，支持包括暗物质探测、中微子物理、核天体物理等重大科学项目。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统性地介绍了SURF设施的历史发展、物理特征（如4850英尺深度）、基础设施（包括LBNF/DUNE等大型实验平台）、超低本底环境能力、低本底分析技术以及电铸铜生产能力。

Result: SURF已成功运营多年，成为多个重大实验的所在地，包括LUX-ZEPLIN暗物质实验、MAJORANA DEMONSTRATOR无中微子双贝塔衰变实验、CASPAR核天体物理实验，并正在建设LBNF/DUNE中微子实验设施。

Conclusion: SURF是一个关键的地下研究基础设施，为多学科前沿科学提供了独特的实验环境。设施持续发展，未来将通过DUNE等大型国际合作项目进一步推动粒子物理、天体物理等领域的研究进展。

Abstract: The Sanford Underground Research Facility (SURF) began operation in 2007 as a facility dedicated to advancing compelling multidisciplinary scientific research. SURF is one of the deepest laboratory sites and offers the largest footprint in the world for scientific pursuits, including physics campuses on the 4850-foot level where the LUX-ZEPLIN, MAJORANA DEMONSTRATOR, and CASPAR experiments are located. SURF is also home to the Long-Baseline Neutrino Facility (LBNF) that will host the international Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE). SURF provides ultra-low background environments, low-background assay capabilities, and electroformed copper is produced at the facility. In this review, we discuss the history, features and status of the facility, as well as the current scientific program and future evolution and plans.

</details>


### [124] [Modern jet flavour tagging in hadronic Z decays with archived ALEPH data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06524)
*Matteo M. Defranchis,Jacopo Fanini,Apranik Fatehi,Gerardo Ganis,Taj Gillin,Loukas Gouskos,Luka Lambrecht,Michele Selvaggi,Birgit Stapf*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 对ALEPH实验LEP数据的重新分析，应用现代喷注味标记技术改进Z玻色子强子衰变道的分离，相比传统算法将b/c夸克喷注误判率降低一个数量级，首次实现LEP数据中奇异夸克喷注标记，为未来电子-正电子对撞机提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用现代喷注味标记技术重新分析LEP存档数据，改进Z玻色子强子衰变道的分离能力，为精确电弱测量提供更高质量的数据样本，并为未来对撞机实验提供技术参考。

Method: 应用现代喷注味标记技术，结合寿命信息、粒子识别和次级顶点信息，采用基于深度学习的分类器；首次实现LEP数据中奇异夸克喷注标记；使用tag-and-probe方法在数据中校准标记器。

Result: 相比传统算法，在相同识别效率下，b/c夸克喷注误判率降低一个数量级；首次实现LEP数据中奇异夸克喷注标记；所有夸克味的数据与模拟一致性良好；为改进电弱精密观测量测量铺平道路。

Conclusion: 现代喷注味标记技术显著提升LEP存档数据的分析能力，为改进电弱精密测量提供重要工具，并为未来电子-正电子对撞机的探测器和算法开发提供指导。

Abstract: We present a reanalysis of archived data from the ALEPH experiment at LEP in the $\mathrm{Z \to q\bar{q}}$ final state. We apply modern jet flavour tagging techniques to improve the separation between the different hadronic decay channels of the Z boson, achieving up to one order of magnitude improvement in misidentification rate for b- and c-quark jets compared to the legacy algorithms used for the most recent ALEPH results, for the same identification efficiency. We also present the first implementation of strange quark jet tagging with LEP data, which allows for the selection of a $\mathrm{Z \to s\bar{s}}$ enriched event sample. These improvements in the flavour tagging performance are achieved by leveraging the lifetime, particle identification, and secondary vertex information, as well as modern classifiers based on a deep learning approach. We also demonstrate the calibration of the tagger in data using a tag-and-probe method, obtaining good data to simulation agreement for all quark flavours. These results pave the way for improved measurements of electroweak precision observables with LEP archived data, and can serve as a guidance for the development of detectors and algorithms for future electron-positron colliders.
